ParlaMint-AT_2000-11-23-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00045_d7e2739	Please, Minister of State.
ParlaMint-AT_2000-11-23-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00045_d7e2754	Deputies! They know that the so-called HG-STRAN, i.e. an adjustment to the 1992 military division, was already in place in 1998, containing in-depth structural measures. These measures have been completed by the summer of this year. Only a few key points: the reduction of the corps command from three to two, the reduction of tank guard brigades from three to two, a reduction in the administration of military command by 30 percent and also a streamlining of organizational measures and school and office divisions. In addition, my objective is to present a reform project, which is already working on a project group, in particular with regard to the top-level integration of the Federal Ministry of Defence. I expect the results of this project group at the end of the year, and we will then start with much Elan to implement a reduced top ranking.

There was a discussion between me and the Minister of Finance. My aim would have been to obtain, as it were, a general clause for all these additional costs. The Minister of Finance wanted to make it clear that there is no additional funding, which, of course, is understandable from his point of view. We agreed that we will only be able to evaluate whether the contributions reported by the Member States are sufficient, whether they are too many, or perhaps they are in wrong areas, if this evaluation is completed – after all, it is only now being evaluated within the framework of the European Union, whether the contributions made by the Member States are sufficient, whether they are too many or if they are perhaps in wrong areas – that we can make our concrete contribution, that we can then also cover the annual tangents of the necessary procurement and the necessary measures in the field of personnel. And then there will be negotiations on the additional funding of this project.

ParlaMint-AT_2000-11-23-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00045_d7e5265	Mr. Federal Minister! What steps do you take to achieve recognition as a university institution for the State Defense Academy? What will you do to ensure that HUAK training is also recognized as vocational training?
ParlaMint-AT_2000-11-23-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00045_d7e5281	Please, Mr. Minister.
ParlaMint-AT_2000-11-23-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00045_d7e5296	Deputies! A working group is currently in use. I have initiated a whole series of such reform projects at the beginning of my duties, or, if they have already been, I have tried to speed them up. We will now come into a first phase in the next few weeks and months, respectively. One of these working groups is concerned with the question that further officer training at the State Defense Academy should also have a university character. This is not quite easy, because of course a wide range of criteria must be met here. But this will also be assessed in the first half of next year. At the Unteroffischersakademie, I believe, a quantum leap has already been made in the assessment of the under-offering training through the conversion of the army sub-offering school into an academy. And in some areas, such as health education, in other fields of vocational training, it has also been possible to offer comparable and recognised training in the civil field. We are now also in the process of allowing access to other civil training steps, for example through professional maturity examinations.

ParlaMint-AT_2000-11-23-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00045_d7e14299	Mr President. Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen. This obviously very serious scandal around the Bank Burgenland, which we were able to address yesterday in the course of the current hours, is not only a criminal case but also a political scandal. This is not only a massive problem for the country of Burgenland, but also a disastrous signal for the saver, for the small investor, for securing the deposits of the savers and the small investors, and hence for protecting the interests of the savers in that country. [[Congratulations with the liberals and the ÖVP.]] Therefore, it must be our concern to restore confidence to the saver, to restore security to him, to be entitled to believe in a stable, solid banking landscape in Austria. That is why I think it is crucial that clear messages be given by this House, clear signals that it must be carefully identified, and that this case should be clarified without delay, that the responsibilities in the political, legal, criminal and also responsibilities must be felt and consequences must be identified and, above all, learn from this question. Again: It is better to have private entrepreneurs than state owners, and therefore it was reasonable and wise that we sold the last bank operating in the banking business, P.S.K., for nearly 18 billion schilling and thus gave the taxpayer an advantage – unlike the country of Burgenland, where more than 4 billion schilling damage to the country and thus to the taxpayer. This is the further proof to say that a private businessman with money with his heart's blood is better than a state entrepreneur. [[Congratulations with the liberals and the ÖVP.]] Ladies and gentlemen! I can therefore come to the specific questions raised. 1. Question: How do you assess the risk of the country of Burgenland, which can be expected due to the acceptance of liability? I should like to try to provide some information in advance so that the future burdens of the country of Burgenland can then be further addressed from the assumed liability commitments. The operating result of Bank Burgenland was 475 million schilling in 1999. The operating result, we all know, I think, is the amount available to a bank from the revenues of the financial year for the formation of risk precautions, which are individual value adjustments, which are provisions. In the 1999 financial year, Bank Burgenland had to make risk precautions of around 500 million Schilling – at least more than the then operating profit of 475 million Schilling. At the time, therefore, reserves and silent reserves had to be dissolved. That is why I am saying this in order to be able to draw a picture of the bank's profitability. As regards Bank Burgenland's profitability, the Management Board of the Bank itself notes in its 1999 Management Report on the financial year 2000 that the projected objective of achieving a operating profit of 310 million schilling – 475 million in 1999, 310 million as a target in 2000 – is particularly ambitious in the light of recent developments. It is therefore, of course, difficult to make statements now as to what acceptance of liability the country of Burgenland will actually take. But as a worst-case scenario, I am allowed to represent yesterday's loads. First point: Hom-Rusch-Guarantee: 2.35 billion schilling. In addition, there is an additional Hom-Rusch risk of 400 million schilling, which has been evaluated in recent months. In addition, the other risk precautions amount to 1.35 billion schilling, which now have to be formed on the basis of credit risk checks, and additional risk precautions for previously unaudited loans are added. In other words, it is now clear that 4.1 billion schilling is needed, and I can add that a framework agreement has been concluded between Burgenland, Bank Austria and the Bank of Burgenland on the occasion of the additional need for value adjustment which was made apparent in October 2000 with a view to preventing the bank's over-indebtedness and giving the country a liquidity guarantee for the Bank of Burgenland. In order to ensure the fulfilment of this liquidity guarantee, Bank Austria is ready to provide the country with credit lines so that it can grant a shareholder loan to the bank or the country assumes the responsibility for direct liquidity assurance by Bank Austria. In any event, the Bank Burgenland will not be able to cover all the claims of the country from the acceptance of liability due to its earnings situation. The final result – of course we will only be able to see this in the future – depends on several variables: 1. Point: What are the defaults for the bank from the credit complex Hom-Rusch? 2. Point: What possible return on bankruptcy is there from the credit complex Hom-Rusch? 3rd. Point: What are the real risks of those who are now manifest with their 1.35 billion schilling? 4. Point: What are the risks arising from the examination of loans that have not been evaluated so far to the extent of 15.4 billion schilling? 5. Point: The interest rate of the country's liability for the credit risks Hom-Rusch and the interest rate of Bank Austria's capital will be an essential fact. 6. Point: The costs of the country from the liquidity guarantee granted to the Bank of Burgenland play a role. 7. Point: of course, the profit situation of Bank Burgenland itself if you want to reduce the country's liability. 8. Point: Increases in capital by third parties that could reduce the country's liability from the credit complex Hom-Rusch are at least disputable. In summary, ladies and gentlemen, from today's point of view: In any case, a very substantial proportion of the 4.1 billion Schilling will meet the country of Burgenland, the Burgenland taxpayer. 2. Question: How do you assess the impact of the Bank-Burgenland scandal on public budgets and in particular on the Internal Stability Pact? It is clear from the fact that the national budget will, of course, be directly burdened when it comes to the impact of the country's liability. As said, the probability is very, very high. There is also, of course, an indirect burden on the national budget, because there is a loss of value in the participation. P.S.K. is an example that shows that a participation can be worth 18 billion schilling. The fact that this bank can no longer be very valuable is clear in view of the recent events. In this respect, one could of course only achieve a very low sales revenue in any privatisation process. This, of course, also damages the wealth situation of the country of Burgenland. We assume that Bank Burgenland will not generate any revenues in the next few years and therefore no revenue can be expected from the title Corporate Tax Payments. On the impact on the Austrian Stability Pact: They know that there is a valid Stability Pact based on the valid financial compensation in 2001. We have negotiated a new agreement with the countries present to the National Council for Consultation and Decision-making and provides for considerable support from the countries for the general budget. For the country of Burgenland this is an amount of 659 million schilling annually, which one will have to do within the meaning of the new Stability Pact. I assume that since the head of state Stix was one of the main negotiators, he is sure that the country will be able to fulfil these commitments, which he has now entered into as head of state and financial referents. 3rd. Question: Is it true that, in the case of the use of the liability of the country of Burgenland, it is also not possible to exclude direct effects on taxpayers by means of tax increases? If so, why? For me, of course, it is primarily a question of budgetary policy: How does it affect taxpayers? Is it going through at all? And how can we allocate funds to the liability case? Therefore, the current budgetary situation of the country is important in order to be able to answer this question. I must say that it is not possible to cover greater liability amounts from the budget, as it is currently happening. So you can't cover it out of surpluses. They all know possible ways to spend money, whether the benefit reductions, asset sales or tax increases are: In any case, the taxpayer will have to pay the invoice at Bank Burgenland. 4. Question: Is the Bank Burgenland scandal at risk of national co-financing of the Burgenland Objective 1 area? If so, how? If no, why not? If a country is in a very difficult budgetary situation, it is clear, of course, that significant financial commitments are very difficult to achieve. That is to say, with this scandal, the country's budgetary situation is facing a new challenge in order to formulate it entirely diplomatically. If you see that the country of Burgenland has to make 522 million schilling from national funds over a 7-year period for Objective 1 funding – in addition, federal funds amounting to 721 million schillings over the entire period – and in addition there is an additionality programme between the federal government and the country, which is financed purely nationally, and the country has to finance another 215 million schillings, the federal government 300 million schilling, then in this context it is clear that ensuring co-financing for the Objective 1 area will be very difficult. 5. Question: Are special arrangements planned by the Federal Minister for Finance in the case of the use of the liability of the country of Burgenland to support this country? If so, which one? I should like to refer once again to the 1997 financial compensation and to the financial compensation now to be concluded. It is clear that all measures, whether they are distribution, tax rights, tax receipts for the federal, state and local authorities, are complementary financial compensation schemes, financial allocations, targeted grants, regulatory content of the financial compensation. In addition, of course, every country has complete autonomy in its budget management, so it is also politically responsible within the framework of the federal laws and the state laws. Therefore, from the Federal Ministry of Finance's point of view, no special arrangements are planned to support the country of Burgenland in the case of the use of the country's liability. They are not possible either because they would have a prejudicial effect for all countries that would simply not be feasible, and would also call into question the self-responsibility and autonomy of a country and thus federalism. 6. Question: How was the intervention of bank supervision in the case of the Bank Burgenland made concrete? You know that we have the possibility to conduct on-the-spot checks at banks within the meaning of § 70 Banking Law. On the one hand, it was a routine test program, which was in front of the door, on the other hand, it was strikingly high value adjustment needs in recent years that led the Federal Ministry of Finance to instruct the Oesterreichische Nationalbank to carry out an on-the-spot check at Bank Burgenland after a corresponding recommendation from the expert commission. This on-the-spot examination then gave rise to the known deficiencies, in contradiction with the bank which has been disputing for a long time, and finally made it possible to detect the Bank-Burgenland scandal. 7. Question: Why did the banking supervision not take the repeated media reports and the warnings just the internal revision of the Bank Burgenland since 1994 to give rise to earlier action? The auditors appointed to the Bank Burgenland have always given full confirmation of the annual accounts and, otherwise, no dramatic or even existential situation of the Bank Burgenland was recognizable from the reports available or collected from the Supervisory Board. The internal revision addressed in the question is an organ of the Executive Board in Austria within the meaning of the Banking Act and also reports to the Executive Board and not to the Banking Supervisory Board. Nor is there a reporting obligation on banking supervision. It was also not deduced from other information on banking supervision, in particular from the reports of auditors, that there are critical reports of internal revision. Therefore, there has been no evidence for banking supervision to intervene earlier than it actually did. 8. Question: On the basis of the experience of the Socialist Bank-Burgenland scandal in the area of banking supervision, will you have consequences? If so, which one? If no, why not? I can see that in the present case, the system of banking supervision has safely failed at several levels. Neither the bank auditors, two renowned auditors, nor the board of directors, nor the Supervisory Board of the Bank-Burgenland have pointed to the existing problems, let alone put forward preventive measures in advance, which, as I said yesterday, also applies to the owner of the Bank, who was represented by the Bank by October 1998 in the form of government commissioners who appointed the country. That means that there has also been an blatant failure of the owner, the owner's representative and thus this political responsibility. I ask for understanding that I do not derive any consequences for banking supervision from a "socialist bank-bourgeois scandal", as it is stated in the question, but it has already given a consensus, in the context of the Government Convention, entirely independent of the policy, and can be demonstrated in advance, to establish an independent banking supervision on an international basis. We are in the process of implementing what is set out in the Government Convention, have commissioned an international team of experts, are in negotiations and will bring about a major throw for independent banking supervision according to international model. [[Congratulations with the liberals and the ÖVP.]] 9. Question: How do you assess the fact that the socialist head of state Stix has not responded to warnings for years and even after the OeNB report has been announced, has not prevented the debts of the Hom-Rusch complex from increasing by another 422 million schilling? I say quite frankly that it is not my duty to judge or comment on the behaviour of the head of state. However, it is clear from the example of Bank Burgenland that the exercise of the ownership obligations by the public authorities did not take place that the ownership role was not sufficiently perceived. In particular, it is difficult for the country of Burgenland to be liable for all the liabilities of the bank in the event of its insolvency, and therefore the right to have at all times a special view, the audit, the audit rights, which were also not exercised. That is why I made my clear statement yesterday: There is, of course, not only the criminal case, but also, above all, the political responsibility which has been demonstrated by the non-perception of property rights. 10. Question: How do you judge the fact that the financial procuratorate was still on the 12th? Despite the existence of a rich bank-bourgeois report by the Austrian National Bank on May 2, 2000, did the Bank of Burgenland have an obligation to advertise? In view of the Authority's obligation to notify § 84 StPO in the event of notification of a criminal act to be prosecuted by its own authority, I have requested the Financial Procuratorate to make a careful statement on the OeNB report. In addition to the OeNB report, in order to give the financial lawyer a comprehensive picture for the assessment of the facts, I also submitted the legal opinion of the Bank on this and the draft opinion of the bank auditor. I should like to point out that these reports had different points of view at the time. After the examination of the documents submitted, the Financial Procuratorate, with a letter dated 12 March, has been sent to the Court of Auditors. May informs that there is not a sufficiently concrete substantial suspicion of action and therefore does not contain an advertisement obligation of the Federal Ministry of Finance pursuant to § 84 StPO. Of course, the officials of my department immediately notified the prosecutor's office after the 2nd meeting. In June 2000, the first balance sheet falsifications at Howe AG and the Allhauer Holzwerke became known. Questions 11 to 16, including 16, relate exclusively to questions subject to the obligation to pay and to confidentiality. If so, in what context? On the basis of the documents before me and as I am told, there were no further interventions in the Federal Ministry of Finance. Thanks so much for the attention.

ParlaMint-AT_2000-11-23-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00045_d7e23186	Mr President. Mrs. Federal Minister! High house! I would like to make another amendment in connection with the budget accompanying law. The amendment is relatively comprehensive, so the President was asked that the request be made in accordance with § 53 para. 4 GOG is distributed. The President-in-Office of the Council has kindly complied with this request, so I would like to limit myself to explaining the request in the key points. Ladies and gentlemen! The amendment concerns four amendments: First of all, a change to the Income Tax Act: This is essentially a clarification in point 23 litera b concerning disbursement of 13th and 14th respectively. Reference is made to the fact that a provisional wage tax deduction is not allowed. In this context, special tax arrangements for the exclusion of tasks of the local authorities are also concerned: It is hereby clarified that lease and lease contracts concluded between legal persons under public and private law as owners or as tenants are free of charge. Furthermore, there should be content clarifications and editorial changes in the ASFINAG Act and in the Federal Forest Act. The ASFINAG Act redefines a road path regarding the federal road 100 or the estuary into the south motorway. Ladies and gentlemen! Another change concerns the housing law on non-profit-making. It has already been discussed in the course of the debate today: He is concerned that the Ministry of Finance would like to withdraw the orderly withdrawal – I expressly emphasize: the ordered withdrawal – from the housing area. The amendments made here therefore look complicated, because in paragraphs 1 and 2 to 7 a technical crunch is necessary: The non-profit-making housing companies of the public bodies – that is, the federal government, the countries and the municipalities – first grant their tenants a purchase option. This is already in the previous version of the Budgetary Accompaniment Act. As a technical crunch, the amendment proposal now provides that the global provisions of the Housing Genuineness Act will temporarily be repealed, but they will be put into effect immediately by means of an opt-in on the part of the housing companies, limited to 30 days. ==References====External links== In other words, non-profit housing companies, who continue to want to fully claim non-profit, not only in respect of the housing regime, can apply immediately at the office of the state government. With a few – probably only five housing companies, namely those owned by the federal government – the public utility character will be abolished globally, while the provisions of the law of law will not be repealed. Ladies and gentlemen! Both procedures, as I believe, represent an equivalent clear ecological improvement towards the deposition without prior treatment. In particular, it has been demonstrated that mechanical-biological waste treatment plants reduce the greenhouse load compared to the storage without pre-treatment as much as waste combustion plants. Nevertheless, representatives of the Ministry of the Environment are obviously of the opinion that it is necessary to act as a birth aid for the widespread use of waste incineration plants. The current ALSAG novelling provides that no ALSAG contribution is payable for the residues of the waste burnings, while the landfill fractions of the mechanical biological plants ALSAG are required. Ladies and gentlemen! This inequality does not make sense from an ecological or economic point of view. Ecologically, the procedures are equivalent. This cannot be a reason for favouring one of the two technologies. Economically, mechanically-biological waste treatment is cheaper, as the machine-technical effort of waste incineration plants is enormously high. Ladies and gentlemen! It is understandable that there must be a pot for old load exploration and collection. However, it is not possible to explain why this should only be financed by MBA waste. Ladies and gentlemen! That is why I would like to ask the Minister for the Environment to give the MBA a chance again! Thank you.

And the highlight of the whole thing is that such people will no longer arrive at these apartments, because if these apartments belong to private real estate entrepreneurs, then they will no longer be socialized in the rental. This cannot be done by private entrepreneurs, otherwise they will not be able to manage the capital they need to raise these 30 billion schillings on the capital market. This is quite simply not to be understood by the business community! Ladies and gentlemen! Those who do not buy from tenants under time pressure now have to expect a clear rental increase in the second step. This is the message we are proclaiming here! Only the conservation and improvement contributions can be significantly increased under the existing WGG.
